## §R14 — 17-3d preflight (2026-10-18, HEAD `39cd7966`)

Filed under rule 9 at 17-3d's party-mode preflight (operator request: *resolve the issues raised in the story creation for the long-term correctness*). Four read-only reviewers re-read the ratified contract against the tree (production source unchanged since creation). Rulings D-17-3d-PF-R1…R4 are recorded in `17-3d-flag-winston-decision.md` §Preflight rulings; story §Preflight carries PF-01…PF-12.

| # | Finding (OLD → NEW) | Lands on |
|---|---|---|
| **R17-157** | **RULED (PF-R1). OLD (§17-3d AC3; ADR-060 §Amendment 2026-09-26; R17-46's 17-3d share; the 17-3d clauses of the *Kernel-Δ* paragraph and the *Makes true* line):** *a connected socketpair per spawn, the runner's end inherited; `sendto`/`recvfrom` in T2; the `SandboxSpec` field and its `pre_exec` CLOEXEC clear; Yama as a deployment condition.* **NEW:** recall rides the runner's ADR-032 stdio in-band — one CBOR-tagged request record on stdout during an active export, one tagged reply record on stdin, dispatched by maos-bin's session loop, which owns the stdin writer and reads every stdout record since 17-3c (`crates/maos-kernel-core/src/lifecycle/cli_wrapper/runtime.rs:836-860`; `crates/maos-bin/src/spirit_session.rs:330-457`). The 2026-09-04 rejection of in-bridge recall rested on +80–150 kernel lines (`preflight-r2/epic-17.md:183`) that no longer exist. No descriptor, socket, syscall, `unsafe` or `libc`; the T2 allow-list and exit line 3 are unchanged; the recall client is std-only. ADR-060 amended (`:230-268`). **Prototyped the same day** in an isolated copy (`implementation-artifacts/17-3d-evidence/inband/`): through the real kernel bridge under real T2 (`Seccomp: 2`), 6/6 scenarios incl. hostile reply, EOF and a `@2.0` guest; kernel 26311 / 20545 unchanged; `unsafe` 0; `spawned_scope_17_3c` 22/22; `maos-wasm-host` +286 tokei (guests count toward its row). | 17-3d AC3; ADR-060; 17-6 hand-off withdrawn |
| **R17-158** | **RULED (PF-R2). OLD (R17-155):** *the scheduler's concrete-adapter recall seam is dormant and stays untouched.* **NEW:** deleted — `KernelCtx.log_recall`, `HookDispatcher`'s field and builder, `SpiritSchedulerAdapter::new`'s parameter (`kernel_ctx.rs:27,48,85-88`; `hook_dispatch.rs:60,79,117-120,167-169`; `scheduler_loop.rs:110,127-129`); production stops passing the adapter (`main.rs:2730`). FW-01 becomes `scheduler_loop.rs` + deletion-only `hook_dispatch.rs`/`kernel_ctx.rs`; FW-02 **no raise** (≤ 26311 / ≤ 20545, exact lower at landing). | 17-3d AC3.2, T1, Delivery gate |
| **R17-159** | **NEW:** recall drops only kind 7 (`log_recall.rs:338-344`) and fetch checks only the pid (`:481`), so a Spirit could recall and fetch its own refusal receipts (with `owner_pid`), 17-3c's `spirit.frame.authorization` rows and the SSO issuer's `identity.asserted` rows — kind 30, which `FrameKind::from_i64` does not know and readers relabel `TaskAssign` (`transparency_log.rs:134-168`, `:1564-1572`). **NEW contract:** one raw-kind disclosure allow-list (bus frames 0–6 and `McpInvocation` 18), in SQL before `LIMIT`, shared by recall, fetch and the cross-wall reader; anything else is `ScopeViolation`. 17-8's participant scope widens whose frames, never which kinds. | 17-3d AC3.4; 17-8 AC2 |
| **R17-160** | **NEW:** `codec` charges 1 KiB of a 16 MiB expansion budget per CBOR node (`crates/maos-wasm-host/src/codec.rs:29-50`), so serde byte arrays make a 16 KiB payload unencodable; recall materialized every payload (`transparency_log.rs:1473-1477`); `u64` timestamps wrapped through `as i64` (`:1498-1511`); no index served `(boot, pid, keyset)`. **NEW:** byte strings, both reply budgets, longest-fitting page prefix, `recall-error::reply-too-large(u64)` in `@2.1.0`; metadata-only recall; timestamps ≤ `i64::MAX`; `idx_tlog_boot_pid_ts`. | 17-3d AC2.2, AC3.4, AC3.6 |
| **R17-161** | **RULED (PF-R3). NEW:** every mint charges a per-pid issuance quota that never resets in production (`capability/mod.rs:188-190`; `cap_quota/mod.rs:118-123` has only a test caller), and the Researcher re-walks and re-fetches its whole history each idle tick (`spirits/researcher/src/lib.rs:439-449`) — with FW-09 A's per-call minting, quadratic issuance until every capability is denied. 17-3d makes the Researcher walk incremental and proves exhaustion is typed; the window itself → **new story `17-9-capability-issuance-quota-window`** (after 17-8), which also takes `deferred-work.md:1076`. The native port gains `LogRecallError::CapabilityDenied` (one error mapping for both forms). | 17-3d T6, T8; §17-9; `deferred-work.md:1076` |
| **R17-162** | **NEW:** 17-8 makes received frames participant-visible, so the `--once` seed fetch will succeed: exit line 2b's R3 row and 17-3d's AC4.1 safety vector stop holding. The TS scaffold accepts success or `scope-violation` (no rethrow), and 17-8 AC1 re-states line 2b's rows and proven-red and replaces the safety vector with its consent vectors. | 17-3d AC1.2, T7; 17-8 AC1 |
| **R17-163** | **RULED (PF-R4). OLD (FW-04):** *cross-boot recall by a stable identity is out of scope* (no owner). **NEW:** a non-goal by design, written into ADR-013 (`:25-27`): local recall is incarnation-bound; persistence across restarts is the memory tiers (FR28) and distillates (FR30); revisit only with a verified Spirit identity. | ADR-013; `requirements-inventory.md:557` |
| **R17-164** | **NEW (planning consistency):** the story table and §17-3d *Closes · Δ* still said 17-3d closes FR29 for the non-Rust form and that no later-epic story waits on it (20-7 AC4's recall leg does, R17-154); §17-8 AC4 let creation *decide* whether to drop ADR-013's header-only elision although FW-07 A assigned it; §17-8's ACs had no command. **NEW:** the table and *Closes · Δ* read "FR29 form parity" with the 20-7 dependency named; §17-8 AC1 is a command, AC5 delivers header-only elision (dropping it needs a superseding ruling and an ADR-013 amendment), AC6 names its fixtures. | Story table; §17-3d; §17-8 |

## §R15 — 17-4 creation (2026-10-19, HEAD `c49032a1`)

Filed under rule 9 at 17-4's creation. Four read-only scouts (launch surface; kernel hooks and telemetry; Observer, corpus and requirements; governance and CI) re-read every §17-4 cite; two non-author validators checked the draft. Rulings CR-1…CR-6 are creation defaults recorded in the story (the operator may overrule them before dev); D-17-4-FW-01 (R17-174) was ruled by the operator at creation.

| # | Finding (OLD → NEW) | Lands on |
|---|---|---|
| **R17-165** | **OLD (AC1):** *`maos run spirits/observer/manifest.toml` alongside Butler.* **NEW:** two `maos run` processes share no scalar stream — each root builds its own `TelemetryStreamAdapter` (`crates/maos-bin/src/main.rs:1911`; per-process state `crates/maos-kernel-core/src/telemetry/mod.rs:35-43`); `maos run` takes one manifest (`worker_spawn.rs:75-87`); topology refuses Butler (`main.rs:4564-4569`). Observer is the serving root and Butler joins it through the door (`maosctl load` stops at `Loaded`, `main.rs:8805-8857`; `maosctl start`), whose Butler arm is faithful (`main.rs:825-843`). | 17-4 AC1, CR-1 |
| **R17-166** | **OLD (AC1, R17-1, R17-2):** enum `:444-453`, classify `:456-468`, refusal `:3702/:4091`; parity tests iterate both ways. **NEW:** enum `main.rs:438-449`, classify `:451-464`, exhaustive matches `:810-844` / `:4496-4570` / `:4976-5557`, door `matches!` `:8820-8822`, `KNOWN_CLASS_NAMES` `admission.rs:344-353`, gate 5 `:494-501`. `story_16_6_known_class_names_match_classify_spirit` is one-directional (`main.rs:13525-13539`); the round-trip list has 7 names, Digest already missing (`:13695-13723`) — repaired at origin. One `construct_observer` for every arm. | 17-4 AC1.5–AC1.6, CR-2 |
| **R17-167** | **OLD (AC2):** *pump bridges `TelemetryStreamAdapter` to `fire_on_telemetry_event`.* **NEW:** topics are per exact tag, no wildcard; `subscribe` is `None` until `subscribe_topic` created the channel and a publish with no channel or receiver is dropped (`telemetry/mod.rs:79-126`). The pump subscribes `scalar.tap.<tag>` for every `[[epistemic_policy.rules]].tag` of each compiled-in Spirit at admission, before start, acquiring one receiver per topic synchronously (`admission.rs:659-762`); the WASM arm shares `load_admit_start` (`main.rs:5544-5584`), so registration is keyed on the constructed class, never on `forms`. Undeclared tags are not observed. The hook gets bytes (`maos-spirit-abi/src/lifecycle.rs:73-76`); payload = the event's JSON with `serde_json/float_roundtrip` (plain parsing is not bit-exact for every finite `f64`). Delivery is released in `seq` order (R17-174), sequential, Running-only; lag, ineligible, late and failed deliveries are counted, never silent. | 17-4 AC2, CR-4, CR-5 |
| **R17-168** | **OLD (AC1):** *Observer prints live events.* **NEW:** Observer emits nothing (`spirits/observer/src/lib.rs:453-475`) and watches nothing by default (`:477-488`). An activity sink is injected; maos-bin prints one JSON line per received scalar from inside the hook. | 17-4 AC1.2, T1 |
| **R17-169** | **OLD (AC3):** *idle-cadence caller in `scheduler/idle_watchdog.rs`*, kernel-Δ 0. **NEW:** that file is kernel source with a pinned digest (`xtask/src/check_kernel_baseline.rs:7-17`), and IdleWatchdog fires once per quiescent epoch (`idle_watchdog.rs:93-96`) — telemetry never refreshes `last_inbound_frame_ns`, so a passive Observer would consolidate once per lifetime. A maos-bin `ConsolidationWatchdog` fires `fire_on_consolidate` every `[scheduling].idle_window_ms` while the Spirit is Running and inbound-quiescent; kernel untouched. | 17-4 AC3, CR-3 |
| **R17-170** | **OLD (AC4):** thresholds via NFR-Obs-3; emitter list `(seq, tag, ts)`. **NEW:** the ≤ 2 % / ≥ 99 % floors are `prd/project-scoping-phased-development.md:145`; NFR-Obs-3 (`non-functional-requirements.md:103`) is the substrate only. Identity is the store-assigned `seq` (R17-174); causal pairs are consecutive same-emitter events across **all** tags (independent emitters carry no cross-causality); missed must equal the pump's reported lag, behind a hard drain barrier (no silent loss); a zero denominator fails. The corpus lives in `tests/corpora/` under `MANIFEST.toml` (`check-corpus`), and FR55 / NFR-Obs-3 coverage rows (`tests/coverage-matrix.yaml:780-790`, `:1165-1169`) list it in `corpora`. | 17-4 AC4 |
| **R17-171** | **OLD (Δ line, R17-20):** maos-bin +80–150; roster 24 → 26. **NEW:** `maos-bin = 26340` at zero headroom (`xtask/kloc.toml:510-511`), aggregate 177609 / 177610 (`:708-709`) — every line is a measured, operator-approved request before landing; +80–150 is a forecast. `SCANNED_SOURCE_FILES` is 27 with a dynamic count (`crates/maos-bin/tests/cohort_daemon_smoke_13_5c.rs:924,1033-1048`) → 28. The registry booking to 17-4 (`kloc.toml:681-682`) was spent by 17-3b and is removed at landing. | 17-4 Delivery gate |
| **R17-172** | **NEW:** *Closes* claims FR55 and the Observer corpus, but the hermetic exit block proved neither and `epic-17-exit` had no Observer step. Exit line 5 and its provenance bullet are added above, owed by 17-4. | exit block (applied), 17-4 AC5 |
| **R17-173** | **NEW:** the WASM world exports only `handle-frame`, `on-start`, `on-shutdown` (`wit/spirit.wit:343-355`) and Workers are CLI processes, so neither hook reaches a spawned form; FR55 closes for in-process Spirits. Growing the WIT needs ratification and has no consumer — a non-goal, not a deferral. | 17-4 docs (AC5.2) |
| **R17-174** | **OLD (§17-4 *Closes · Δ*):** *kernel-Δ 0.* **NEW:** one broadcast channel per tag and a sequence-less, millisecond-stamped `ScalarTapEvent` (`maos-domain/src/invariants/i7.rs:43-54`; `maos-capability/src/working_memory/store.rs:64-78`) make the ≥ 99 % causal floor unmeetable across tags — `A1,B1,A2,B2` can arrive `A1,A2,B1,B2`. **Ruled 2026-10-19 (operator, D-17-4-FW-01):** `seq: u64` assigned per `WorkingMemoryStore` under its write lock; the pump releases by `seq`; the floor covers every same-emitter pair. Kernel-core touches only the two struct literals in `telemetry/mod.rs`'s test module (`:175`, `:199`): ≤ 26293 physical / ≤ 20529 tokei, exact at landing. Narrowing the metric to per-tag streams was declined. | 17-4 AC4.1, Delivery gate |

## §R16 — 17-4 preflight (2026-10-19, HEAD `c49032a1`)

Filed under rule 9 at 17-4's party-mode preflight (operator request: *resolve the issues raised in the story creation for the long-term correctness*). Four independent reviewers; the transport reviewer measured an isolated prototype (`implementation-artifacts/17-4-evidence/preflight/`). Rulings D-17-4-PF-R1/R2 are recorded in `17-4-flag-winston-decision.md`; story §Preflight carries PF-01…PF-14.

| # | Finding (OLD → NEW) | Lands on |
|---|---|---|
| **R17-175** | **RULED (PF-R1). OLD (R17-167, R17-174):** *one receiver per declared `scalar.tap.<tag>` topic plus a time-hold reorder buffer keyed by `seq`.* **NEW:** a stalled forwarder past the hold inverts one emitter's cross-tag order irreparably, and policy tags are not a telemetry schema (any nonempty tag is accepted, `crates/maos-capability/src/working_memory/store.rs:43-62`). `TelemetryStreamAdapter` gains one broad channel + `subscribe_all()`; the pump holds one FIFO receiver. Measured: 1 500 alternating A/B writes in exact order; `Lagged(3)` once at capacity 2; kernel 26291 → 26304 physical, 20527 → 20538 tokei (with FW-01's literals); kernel-surface baseline regenerated (struct hash, `xtask/src/check_service_boundary.rs:539-553`). `seq` = store-write order, an identity label only. | 17-4 AC2.1–2.2, AC4.1, Delivery gate |
| **R17-176** | **NEW:** the serving drain waits 5 s then detaches (`crates/maos-bin/src/main.rs:8480-8500`) while Observer's budget is 10 s — new tasks join without a detaching timeout; registration moves after the admission decision with rollback on start failure (`admission.rs:747-776`). | 17-4 AC2.3, AC2.6, AC3.1 |
| **R17-177** | **NEW:** hot-swap keeps the pid and replaces manifest and object without a class check (`migration_plan.rs:56`; `hot_swap/coordinator.rs:299-316`) — eligibility is decided live per delivery (Running + current class `observer`); quarantine (K panics or one `BudgetExceeded`) is keyed by runtime object. The pause/unload-vs-dispatch window matches IdleWatchdog today and stays FR51(a)'s gap (E16-A6). | 17-4 AC2.3–2.4 |
| **R17-178** | **NEW:** a broad read of every Spirit's scalars wrote no audit row (self-telemetry does, `memory/self_telemetry.rs:187-199`; NFR-Sec-14 lists scalar.tap observation) and Observer's manifest claims a capability grant that does not exist (`spirits/observer/manifest.toml:21-24`) — governance grant/revoke rows via `insert_frame_event`; comment corrected. Tenancy verified: one tenant per root, no team wall crossed. | 17-4 AC2.7 |
| **R17-179** | **NEW:** `ButlerOrchestratorAdapter` ignores the Spirit's pid (D-16-3-N) but forwards its claimed `spirit_id` (`main.rs:598-633`) — host-side identity in every maos-bin scalar port (rule 10(a)). | 17-4 AC2.8 |
| **R17-180** | **NEW:** `println!` in a hook panics on closed stdout and blocks on a full pipe (→ false `BudgetExceeded`) — bounded sink queue + one stdout writer task; window state aggregate-only; stderr tags `escape_debug`. | 17-4 AC2.4, AC2.9, AC3.4 |
| **R17-181** | **NEW:** `[lifecycle].enabled_hooks` accepts 11 of the 14 ABI hook names (`crates/maos-manifest/src/manifest.rs:1536-1550` vs `crates/maos-spirit-derive/src/lib.rs:10-25`); a refused `snapshot` aborts hot-swap, so a Spirit registering explicitly can never be upgraded — fixed at origin (FR55's registration surface). | 17-4 AC3.7 |
| **R17-182** | **RULED (PF-R2) + NEW:** AC5.3 "zero violations" was unmeetable (unrelated rows `tests/coverage-matrix.yaml:242,632,645,796`) → no new violations against a recorded baseline; debt stays with 20-3b. The oracle's drain depended on totals so the silent-loss plant could only time out → `flush()` barrier; a mandatory overflow regression; plants name their red assertion; summary uploaded with provenance. | 17-4 AC4.4–4.7, AC5.1, AC5.3 |

## §R17 — 17-8 creation (2026-10-20, HEAD `1241ce52`)

Filed under rule 9 at 17-8's creation. Five read-only scouts re-read every §17-8 cite and the routed rows R17-148, R17-156, R17-159, R17-162 and R17-164; `kloc-check --json` was run at HEAD; three non-author validators (citations; design and security; tests and governance) checked the draft. Rows marked *pending* were **ruled 2026-10-20** by the operator (Lunarpulse), each as recommended in the story §Rulings: D-17-8-01 A, D-17-8-02 `Full`, D-17-8-03 A, D-17-8-04 A, D-17-8-05 A.

| # | Finding (OLD → NEW) | Lands on |
|---|---|---|
| **R17-183** | **OLD (AC2):** *a side table written in the same transaction as the frame row (precedent `transparency_log.rs:312-319`).* **NEW:** the TL is `crates/maos-iac/src/adapter/transparency_log.rs`; `:312-319` is the base DDL; the retraction side table is written by a separate INSERT (`:1827-1851`); the frame writer is one autocommitted INSERT with the I2 panic on any non-duplicate error (`:885-920`). The atomic precedent is `register_schema_lifecycle`'s manual `BEGIN`/`COMMIT`/`ROLLBACK` (`:1942-1971`). One new routed-write method writes base row + recipients + consent in one transaction; `Duplicate` writes no side row; `approval_log_is_distinct_table` (`:2692-2714`) lists the new tables. | 17-8 AC2.1–AC2.2, T2 |
| **R17-184** | **OLD (AC2):** *recipient identity binds the admitted Spirit.* **NEW:** `FrameAddress` carries no pid (`crates/maos-domain/src/frame.rs:53-59`) and IAC has no resolver; the trusted map is `pid_by_spirit_id` (`crates/maos-bin/src/main.rs:2487-2506`; insert `admission.rs:786-792`, remove `:864-875`). Names are reusable after unload while pids only advance, and DRR is asynchronous: the accepted set is resolved once under one map read **before** the write or enqueue and persisted unchanged; duplicate addresses dedupe by pid. A hot-swapped successor keeps pid and boot and inherits its predecessor's rows (I6). | 17-8 CR-1, AC2.3 |
| **R17-185** | **OLD (AC1, AC2; ADR-013):** *frames addressed by role.* **NEW:** no role fan-out exists — the mailbox routes by `spirit_id` and ignores `role` (`crates/maos-iac/src/adapter/mailbox.rs:330-340`). Participation comes from the resolved `spirit_id`; `role` is recorded, never confers participation (vector V3b). | 17-8 CR-1, AC1 |
| **R17-186** | **NEW:** production installs no `ConsentGate` (`main.rs:2514-2519`); when installed it runs inside `Mailbox::deliver_inner` after the routed TL row is committed (`mailbox.rs:342-368`), and `Mailbox::deliver` is also called directly (`crates/maos-bin/src/delegation.rs:313-317`, kernel `consent_rupture_adr_034.rs`). A *prepared delivery* (gate verdicts + resolved pids, computed once) is journaled with the frame, carried through DRR's `Submission` and consumed by the mailbox; standalone callers prepare internally; ADR-034 order and payloads unchanged. | 17-8 AC2.4 |
| **R17-187** | **OLD (AC3):** *ruptured since delivery → omitted.* **NEW:** no such transition exists — ADR-034 rupture is receiver-side rejection at delivery (`12-architecture-decision-records.md:474-482`); producers are the A2A router's denials (`crates/maos-a2a-core/src/router.rs:421-476`) journaled by `journal_rupture_frame` (`crates/maos-cohort/src/digest.rs:320-364`) and the unused mailbox gate; no grant registry exists. *Pending D-17-8-03* (recommended A: grant-scoped rupture rows keyed by granter + exact intent class + recipient, written with the kind-22 row, matched by insertion order). | 17-8 AC3.5, D-17-8-03 |
| **R17-188** | **OLD (AC5):** *creation measures and ratifies the frame and WIT change header-only needs.* **NEW:** `ConsentEnvelope` has five fields and no recall policy (`frame.rs:430-444`); any byte edit to `wit/spirit.wit` re-issues the four pinned components, and a field in `consent-envelope` changes the exported `handle-frame` type (breaks deployed `@2.x` guests). *Pending D-17-8-01* (recommended A: domain-only `ConsentRecall { Full, HeaderOnly, Withheld }`, daemon-enforced, elided from the lowered envelope, no WIT change; guests observe `payload-available = false` and `consent-withheld`). Default policy of envelopes minted today: *pending D-17-8-02* (recommended `Full`). | 17-8 AC5, D-17-8-01/02 |
| **R17-189** | **OLD (AC3):** *expired = `now_ns > valid_until_ns`, the router's semantics.* **NEW:** the router's clock returns `u64::MAX` pre-epoch (`router.rs:65-71`) and TTLs saturate (`:1029-1046`), so values above `i64::MAX` are real; a clamp to `i64::MAX` inverts the strict comparison. One pure `expired_at` + `consent_wall_now_ns` in `maos-domain` shared with the router; consent timestamps and `now` bound through one order-preserving key `(v ^ (1 << 63)) as i64`; never the TL's `wall_clock_now_ns` (returns 0 pre-epoch, `transparency_log.rs:2231-2238`). | 17-8 AC3.1–AC3.2 |
| **R17-190** | **OLD (AC1, AC6):** *a remote artifact without side tables degrades to emitter scope with a typed marker.* **NEW:** the remote artifact is a foreign-team shard read through the shared `query_page(…, None, …)` (`crates/maos-bin/src/cross_wall_log_read.rs:25-68`); a participant predicate there would fail on every legacy shard with `no such table`. *Pending D-17-8-04* (recommended A: cross-wall stays emitter-scoped per ADR-058 D2 and every cross-wall page carries `RecallScope::Emitter { CrossWall }`, with or without side tables). | 17-8 AC6.1, D-17-8-04 |
| **R17-191** | **OLD (AC6):** *readers include `check_rto`.* **NEW:** every reader names its columns (`maos-audit/src/lib.rs:186-198`, `:368-371`; SIEM; backup Merkle over `frame_id`; loom-lite canonical); the xtask verb is `rto-drill`, not `check-rto`; `check-migration-merkle`'s corpus is pinned by whole-file SHA and stays base-only. `load_ratification_frames` decodes every kind-28 row and selects columns the schema lacks (`maos-audit/src/lib.rs:526-560`) — pre-existing; 17-8 adds no kind-28 shape and records it as a non-goal row. | 17-8 AC6.2, CR-5 |
| **R17-192** | **OLD (AC2):** *direct and DRR paths.* **NEW:** DRR is not installed in production (`with_drr_scheduler` has test callers only); its flush writes serialized lineage into `intent` (`drr_scheduler.rs:303`) — fixed to the coarse intent (rule 10(a)); `retract()` builds a zero frame id and reads the shared `last_frame_id` (`crates/maos-iac/src/adapter.rs:734-737`, `:802-851`) — one id per operation. | 17-8 CR-3, CR-4, AC2.2 |
| **R17-193** | **OLD (AC1):** *native (the Researcher port) through every vector.* **NEW:** compiled-in Spirits are not mailbox-registered (`admission.rs:786-803`); `IacBusPort::deliver` journals at pid 0 (`mailbox.rs:672-691`); MCP, halt, crash, verb-resolver and raw-byte rows are sender-only with no addressees. Native vectors use the real Researcher admission plus a test mailbox handle; native sender recall is proven on its MCP rows; pid-0 native sends are a recorded non-goal. | 17-8 AC1 V1, CR-1, CR-5 |
| **R17-194** | **OLD (R17-162):** *17-8 re-states line 2b and replaces 17-3d's AC4.1.* **NEW (measured scope):** seven `recall_17_3d` vectors change (seed, empty page, cross-boot, own-frame, receipt isolation, consent safety, SSO incl. its child `--exact` string), CI's two `16 passed` guards and name list (`.github/workflows/discipline.yml:3578-3608`), the line-2b step and checker (`:2023-2043`; `scripts/epic_17_exit_line2b.py`), and the TS scaffold, which rethrows everything but `scope-violation` (`examples/example-spirit-ts/src/index.ts:21-38`). | 17-8 AC1, T6, T7, T10 |
| **R17-195** | **NEW:** `consent-withheld` is reserved in WIT and on the wire, but native `LogRecallError` has no such variant (`crates/maos-domain/src/log_recall.rs:340-378`) and mediation never produces it (`crates/maos-bin/src/recall_mediation.rs:192-200`, `:399-412`). `ConsentWithheld { frame_id }` + one receipt `log.fetch.refused:consent-withheld`; fetch order scope → consent → size, so a withheld frame never yields `reply-too-large`. | 17-8 AC2.6, AC3.4 |
| **R17-196** | **OLD (§Closes · Δ):** *kernel-Δ expected 0, measured at creation.* **NEW:** 0 by construction — kernel `src` constructs no `ConsentEnvelope` (only `consent_envelope: None`, `inference/mod.rs:188`, `scheduler/hook_dispatch.rs:237`), domain frame types reach it through an explicit re-export list (`crates/maos-iac/src/adapter/frame.rs:18-22`), and every existing TL writer signature is kept. Out-of-kernel at HEAD (measured): `maos-iac` 7298, `maos-bin` 27323, `maos-domain` 9647, `maos-wasm-host` 2253, aggregate 178625/178626 — all zero headroom; every raise is a measured request before landing (FW-03). | 17-8 §Delivery gate |
| **R17-197** | **Cite drift (no premise change):** consent comment `log_recall.rs:489-497` → `:446`; `ScopeViolation` `:482-487` → `:430-439`; first recipient `adapter.rs:584-595` → `.first()` at `:563` (DRR `:294`); guest restamp `spirit_session.rs:948-960` → `:971-999`; seed `:102-128`/`:356-361` → `:106-132`/`:368-374`; Researcher injection `main.rs:5349-5352` → `:5397-5411`; `FrameFilter` `:213-236` → `:229-254`; `wit_corpus.rs:221-261` (20 records) → `:201-288` (25). | 17-8 cites |

## §R18 — 17-9 creation (2026-10-22, HEAD `3a7c4260`)

Filed under rule 9 at 17-9's creation. Four read-only scouts (kernel quota substrate; minting paths; governance; tests and CI) re-read every §17-9 cite and R17-161; `kloc-check --json` and `check-kernel-baseline` were run at HEAD; one agent measured the E16-A7 prototype K in an isolated copy (`implementation-artifacts/17-9-evidence/creation/`) and its kernel diff was regression-run on a detached worktree; three non-author validators (citations; design and security; tests and governance) checked the draft and every finding was applied; the validated design was re-measured as prototype V2/W (`creation/v2/`). Rows marked *pending* were **ruled 2026-10-22** by the operator (Lunarpulse), each as recommended — D-17-9-FW-01…10 (`17-9-flag-winston-decision.md` §Rulings), by consensus under the operator's decision rule (long-term correctness; PRD strictly; correct architectural drift; honour existing contracts; fill requirement gaps along the roadmap). Under that rule the §4.6 `cap-quota` row's conflation of issuance accounting with ADR-016 is drift, corrected by 17-9 T9, and ADR-016's PRD requirement gets `18-5-context-token-budget-accounting`.

| **R17-199** | **OLD (AC1):** *every issuance within a window's budget succeeds; the first over-budget one is `EContextExhausted`.* **NEW:** the boundary is exclusive today — `ratio >= 1.0` refuses **at** budget (`crates/maos-capability/src/cap_quota/mod.rs:76-85`; 999,999 of 1,000,000 succeed; budget 1 admits nothing). Capacity becomes inclusive with integer thresholds `⌈0.80·B⌉`/`⌈0.95·B⌉`; `EContextExhausted` is the architecture's name for `CapError::ContextExhausted { spirit_id }` (`crates/maos-domain/src/ports/capability.rs:135-137`). | 17-9 AC1, CR-2 |
| **R17-200** | **OLD (AC2):** *a window chosen at creation, time- or turn-based, from one named budget source.* **NEW:** no turn exists across all forms (picker `scheduler_loop.rs:35-74`; `fire_on_frame` uncalled `hook_dispatch.rs:299-301`; idle epochs debounced `idle_watchdog.rs:66-115`; WASM turns maos-bin-only `spirit_session.rs:376-437`; Workers mint per spawn); Story 1b.2's record already fixed a 60 s rollover (`1b-2-…md:1167-1174`) that was never wired; `set_budget` writes a map nothing reads (`cap_quota/mod.rs:41,56-59`). → a 60 s tumbling window on a monotonic clock, one source `DEFAULT_ISSUANCE_QUOTA`. **Ruled 2026-10-22, D-17-9-FW-04 A:** 60,000 per window (measured single-pid ceiling 26,768 issuances/s with real Ed25519). | 17-9 AC2, FW-04 |

Order scope → policy → charge → issue; a failed issue refunds its reservation within its own admission window, and crossings are emitted only by the commit of a successful issue (R17-211); SSO/PDP denials precede the kernel call (never charged); the identity-persistence rollback revokes an issued token (stays charged). | 17-9 AC3, CR-1 |

AC4 needs an emitter and a sink. **Ruled 2026-10-22, D-17-9-FW-05 A:** durable kind-7 `cap.quota:<signal>` rows via cap-audit. | 17-9 AC4, FW-05, CR-7 |

Fixture migrates; native vectors added. | 17-9 AC5.3 |

One library helper writes a kind-28 `mcp.call.refused:capability-denied` receipt on any governed refusal; the live ports gain the journal and trusted id. | 17-9 AC5.4, CR-5 |

Separately, `ScheduleWatchdog` mints its narrowed tokens **before** its rate bucket and continues dispatch after mint failures (`schedule_watchdog.rs:203-217`, `:242`, `:313-345`); its audit record stores the first minted token id or `TokenId::ZERO`, and the hook receives only the schedule payload, never that token (`:255-260`; `control_block.rs:135-143`) — so the narrowed tokens are audit-only, charged against the quota even when the bucket refuses the fire, and no verification of the hook's later capability use follows from them. **Ruled 2026-10-22, D-17-9-FW-10 A:** consume the bucket before minting (move-only); the tokens are documented as an audit attestation, not hook authority. | 17-9 AC5.5, FW-06, FW-10 |

A lazy, activity-driven sweep of strictly older windows in `maos-capability` removes lifetime accumulation without a kernel line (not a fixed-cardinality bound); hot swap keeps the pid and window, cold swap, re-admission and fresh Worker binds start fresh (R17-212). | 17-9 AC2.4, CR-3 |

## Dependencies

Epic 15 (`15-2-kloc-ceiling-rebase` incl. the maos-manifest +150 row and the per-story kernel figures 17-1 +65–130 / 17-2 net ≤+10; `15-5-decision-adrs-and-provisioning-checklist` for ADR-060 (17-3b AC4) and ADR-061 (17-1); `15-3-single-phase-source-and-exit-command-check` for `check-exit-commands` on the exit block). Epic 16 releases the `main.rs` write lock before 17-1 lands (17-1's proxy wiring, 17-3b's launch path and 17-4's Observer arm all edit `main.rs`; `round2.md:33`). Intra-epic: **17-3a before 17-1** (it prices the proxy) **and before 17-3b**; **17-1 before 17-2** (the Worker process model fixes the cgroup layout); 17-4 independent. Outward: 17-1 before `19-3-real-worker-default-with-effect-oracle`'s **operator leg only** (`epic-19-founder-loop-w4.md:60,75`); nothing here needs a later epic.

**⚠ R4 (2026-09-20) — the `main.rs` write-lock clause above is now HALF wrong and is restated per story.** `crates/maos-bin/src/` holds **23 modules** at HEAD (`admission.rs` 16-6, `supervision.rs` 16-3, `purge.rs` 16-4, `operator_door.rs` 16-1, `shell_host.rs` 16-2, `verbs.rs` 15-3, `worker_spawn.rs`, `topology.rs`, …), but `main.rs` is still **14,632 lines** and still exclusively owns the standalone and topology launch matches, `LoadedSpiritKind`/`classify_spirit`, the `TelemetryStreamAdapter` construction and the `spawn_t3` smoke. Therefore: **17-4** is still a `main.rs` launch-block story (enum + three exhaustive matches + the pump's build site) *plus* a one-line `admission.rs:317` edit — the clause understates it; **17-3b**'s launch path now **bifurcates** — gate/admit is `admission.rs` (a `wasm-component` form must be refused-or-routed at `gate_manifest` gate 5, `admission.rs:409`, whose `AdmissionRefusal::UnknownClass` doc at `:221` already names this epic), construct/start is still `main.rs`'s match, and 17-3b's Δ line names `admission.rs` nowhere; **17-1** does **not** contend for the launch block at all — its contact with `main.rs` is the `spawn_t3` smoke at `:6982` only, while its real blast radius is `worker_spawn.rs` / `worker_cli.rs` / **`supervision.rs`** (R17-4). Replace the blanket clause with: *"Epic 16 releases the `main.rs` launch-block lock before **17-4** lands; 17-1 and 17-3b are launch-block-free, but 17-3b acquires `admission.rs`."*

**⚠ R5 (2026-09-25) — one intra-epic story and two edges are added (operator decision, 17-3a RT-8):** the T2 repair (R17-25/R17-26) is **`17-6`**; **17-3a before 17-6** (it prices every kernel line — 17-6 may not leave `backlog` before Q4b is MEASURED) and **17-6 before both 17-2 and 17-3b** (17-2 AC1 needs a live T2 fixture child; 17-3b's runner runs under T2). 17-1, 17-4 and 17-5 are unaffected (17-1's Worker runs under T3, not T2). **`20-3a-gate-honesty` (Epic 20) runs in parallel with 17-6** by operator decision (2026-09-25, D4 — *stop the leaks of failures*); it neither blocks nor waits on an Epic-17 story.

**⚠ R7 (2026-09-26; clarified 2026-10-01 by D-17-3c-ES-01…05) — 17-3b becomes three stories (R17-51):** **17-3b** needs only 17-3a (`done`) and runs in parallel with 17-6; **17-3c** is ordered after **the verified 17-3b contract/admission and refusal snapshot** plus 17-6 (the T2 route). Its preparation does **not** wait for 17-3b to be `done`, because the remaining 17-3b kernel-verdict finding requires 17-3c AC2's ingress; that finding remains open until its own real proof, never waived or transferred. **17-3d** needs 17-3c. Outward: **17-3b → 20-1** (form token, registry admission by form, the componentize-js artifact, the engine-off refusal). 17-3c and 17-3d have no downstream consumer in Epics 18–21. The schema-v5 bump belongs to whichever of 17-1 and 17-3b lands first.
